- **adr**: Create, review, audit, and list Architectural Decision Records for any software project. Not for trivial choices that are cheap to reverse. ([sebmafate/trellis](https://github.com/sebmafate/trellis/tree/HEAD/skills/adr))
- **adr**: Create or update an Architecture Decision Record in docs/adr/ (MADR format). Use whenever a significant design/architecture/scope decision is made, or when the user says "record this decision". ([SelfishCoconut/revalid](https://github.com/SelfishCoconut/revalid/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/adr))
- **adr**: Manage Architecture Decision Records (ADRs) for documenting technical decisions. Use when discussing architecture, design decisions, trade-offs, or when the user mentions ADRs, architectural decisions, or wants to document a technical choice. ([SelrahcD/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/SelrahcD/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/selrahcd-dot-claude/skills/adr))
- **adr**: Creates and maintains Architecture Decision Records (ADRs) following a structured format with State, Context, Decision, Considered, Consequences, Implementation, and References sections. Use when creating, updating, or reviewing architectural decisions, or when the user mentions ADR, architecture decisions, technical decisions, or design records. ([sentenz/template-cpp](https://github.com/sentenz/template-cpp/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/adr))
- **adr**: Create Architecture Decision Records with structured context, rationale, and consequences. Use when a decision is hard to reverse or constrains future work - a database, framework, auth model, deployment topology, or cross-cutting library. ([servika/claude-code-setup](https://github.com/servika/claude-code-setup/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/adr))
- **adr**: Record a decision as an ADR in project/decisions/ — context, options, decision, consequences. Use when a story or the lead makes a choice that changes the architecture, a contract, a tech choice or how lanes work. ([shbunder/marcel](https://github.com/shbunder/marcel/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/adr))
- **adr**: Use when a decision shapes system structure, is expensive to reverse, or needs a durable explanation for future contributors. Also use when the operator asks to write or record an ADR, or when review finds an undocumented architecture choice. Do not use for small in-task choices. ([shiftynick/aigent-place](https://github.com/shiftynick/aigent-place/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/adr) · 2 copies)
- **adr**: ADR（Architecture Decision Record）を起票・更新・Supersede するスキル。 「ADR を起票して」「ADR-000X を書いて」「この設計判断を記録して」「ADR にして」 「ADR を Superseded にして」「既存 ADR と矛盾しないか確認して」などと言われたら使う。 リポジトリ既存の ADR 規約（docs/ADR か docs/decisions か、見出し構成、Status 行の書式、 言語）を自動検出して踏襲し、README の一覧表も更新する。設計変更を伴う実装の前に 「先に ADR を」と判断したときにも積極的に使う。 ([shinobiashi/claude-skills](https://github.com/shinobiashi/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/adr))
- **adr**: Record a technical or product decision and its rationale in Antigravity. Creates an immutable Architectural Decision Record in docs/decisions/ based on the standard template. ([shivamkb17/AntiGravity-Starter-Template](https://github.com/shivamkb17/AntiGravity-Starter-Template/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/adr))
- **adr**: Create or update Architecture Decision Records ([sho7650/tech-news-curator](https://github.com/sho7650/tech-news-curator/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/adr))
- **adr**: rebuilds で ADR（設計の決定の記録）を起票・更新する。題材の領域ごとの番号の範囲から採番し、proposed で書き、一覧を生成し直し、範囲や法令の判断を intent の Open questions へつなぐ。「ADR を書いて」「この決定を ADR にして」や、設計の作業の中で新しい決定が生じたときに使う。 ([shoito/rebuilds](https://github.com/shoito/rebuilds/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/adr))
- **adr**: docs/adr/ 配下のADR(アーキテクチャ決定記録)を新規作成・更新・ステータス変更するときに使う。Nygard形式の書式、テンプレート、ステータス語彙、不変性の運用、一覧の更新手順を扱う。「ADRを書く」「この決定を記録する」「ADRのステータスを変える」「技術選定の根拠を残す」といった作業が該当する。 ([shun968-org/marketing-data-pipeline](https://github.com/shun968-org/marketing-data-pipeline/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/adr))
- **adr**: Use to capture architecture rationale as decision records, backfilling past decisions or writing one for a current decision. ([skylarsabo/code-ops](https://github.com/skylarsabo/code-ops/tree/HEAD/plugins/code-ops-suite/skills/adr) · 2 copies)
- **adr**: Record an architecture decision as a lightweight, immutable ADR in the scope's docs folder. Owns the ADR template, numbering, and write. Invokable directly for a direct-path decision, or by spec-workflow at handoff. ([SlinkyBMajor/ai-playbook](https://github.com/SlinkyBMajor/ai-playbook/tree/HEAD/skills/adr))
- **adr**: Write an Architecture Decision Record (ADR) capturing the decisions made, why they were made, the alternatives considered, and the features or scenarios they relate to. Pulls the content from the current conversation and asks the user for anything missing. Use when the user wants to record, document, or write up a technical or architectural decision, asks for an ADR, or invokes /adr. ([SloanGwaltney/callback](https://github.com/SloanGwaltney/callback/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/adr))
- **adr**: Record an architecture decision record (ADR) in Kinetra or Kinetra-Lab when a decision is hard to reverse, surprising without context and the result of a real trade-off. Use when the user asks for an ADR ("/adr", "leg dit besluit vast"), or when the feature or grilling skill settles such a decision. ([SMB-M87/Kinetra](https://github.com/SMB-M87/Kinetra/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/adr))
- **adr**: Write an Architecture Decision Record: a short, immutable markdown file per significant technical decision, checked into the repo under docs/adr/. Use when the user asks to record or document a decision, or asks why something was chosen. Triggers on "skriv en ADR", "dokumenter denne beslutningen", "hvorfor valgte vi", "write an ADR", "record this decision", "document why we chose". Also propose an ADR when the user makes a decision that clears the bar in this skill, but never write one without asking first. ([sondrejk/dotfiles](https://github.com/sondrejk/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/ai/skills/adr))
- **adr**: Record an architecture decision for a gmod repository - draft it with the person, scope it to the files it governs, write its must and should rules, and leave the yes to them. Use when a decision is made or changed that later work must follow (a data format, a boundary, a library, a rule), or when the person asks for an ADR. ([soundheart-studios/gmod](https://github.com/soundheart-studios/gmod/tree/HEAD/skills/adr) · 2 copies)
- **adr**: Record an architecture, tooling, data-policy or methodology decision as a numbered ADR in docs/decisions/NNNN-&lt;slug&gt;.md. Use when a decision has just been made that a future session might re-debate, or when the human says record this decision or write an ADR. Do not use for bug fixes or routine features (commit bodies and the changelog carry those), or for engineering design decisions with an evidence chain (use the decision skill). ([srinator22/agent-kernel](https://github.com/srinator22/agent-kernel/tree/HEAD/.archive/library-skills/adr))
